# Update Benchmarking Artifacts - backend 9.28.0.45, frontend 5f15ca7

[Upstream PR](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/2088) · state: open · merged: False

## Original PR description

Automated benchmark artifact refresh.

cuDNN backend: 9.28.0.45
cudnn-frontend commit: 5f15ca77ad3bd47c8ffb5070c6d0ab436120fe56

Includes attention_training, attention_inference, dsa, linear_attention and cutedsl_fusion_kernels benchmark artifacts for gb200, gb300, h200, a100 and rtxpro6000 (dsa: gb200/gb300/h200; linear_attention and cutedsl: gb200/gb300).

Also includes a one-hunk fix to `benchmark/attention_inference/charts.py`: the serving-form cases widened some inference figures past the WebP 16383-px limit at the fixed 140 dpi, so three charts per GPU (deepseek_v4 and gpt_oss generation, prefill_sweep context) failed to save. The dpi is now scaled down just enough to fit; the charts in this refresh were produced with that fix.

🤖 Generated with [Claude Code](https://claude.com/claude-code)
